# Palestine House — Project Status

> **The living tracker and source of operation.** Any fresh session — Claude Code, Codex, claude.ai, or a human — reads this file first to know exactly where the build stands and what to do next. It is updated **in the same PR** that completes (or meaningfully advances) a sprint or phase. Plan and exit gates: [`ROADMAP.md`](./ROADMAP.md). Process: [`WORKFLOW.md`](./WORKFLOW.md).

### How to resume in a fresh AI session
1. Read this file, then the active sprint's scope + exit gate in `ROADMAP.md`.
2. Read `CLAUDE.md` (or `AGENTS.md` if you're a review agent) and the docs it points to for the task at hand.
3. Verify the repo state yourself (`package.json`, `src/app/`, `git log`) — trust the code over this file if they disagree, then fix this file.
4. Work only inside the active sprint. Anything else → propose it under §5 or the backlog.
5. Before ending: update §1–§3 here, tick `ROADMAP.md` checkboxes, and include this file in the PR.

Search) · `admin/` (3), plus the locked shared chrome (`shared/site-chrome.*`, `workspace-chrome.*`, `admin-chrome.*`, `pages.css`) and browsable hubs (`index.html`, `WorkspaceIndex.html`). ## 4. Locked decisions (do not reopen)

> ⚠️ **Stage 4 note (updated at PP5, 2026-08-14).** Entries below are kept **as decided, on the date they were decided** — that is the point of this section, and rewriting a past decision would destroy the record. Several of them describe the pre-Stage-4 gated workspace, which **no longer exists**: PP5 deleted it on 2026-08-14. Where an entry conflicts with today, **§5 (D-PP-b / D-PP-c / D-PP-f / D-PP-j) and `ROADMAP.md` Stage 4 win**, and the governing docs (`CLAUDE.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `TECH-ARCHITECTURE.md` §0, `SECURITY-CHECKLIST.md` §15, `DESIGN.md` §8) were rewritten at PP5 to describe the current platform. Superseded here: "only per-user state = `/build` checklist progress" (**dropped**, D-PP-b), the Academy as a live surface (**retired**), the sidebar/stages navigation model (**replaced by the 5-link platform chrome**), and any per-topic card description (**now: summary → one Simple guide card → Watch Video → a many-template grid**, D-PP-f). The public-shell entries are unaffected — the public site was untouched throughout PP.

- **D-DR1 — Public design refresh v3 (owner decision, 2026-07-02 — DR1 on `claude/sprint-dr1-design-v3-home`):** the public shell moves to the **warm, cultural, photography-led v3 system** built from the owner's reference images (`docs/source-assets/design-refs/v3/`). Frontend/design only — no feature/DB/auth/gate/CSP/routing change. The binding sub-decisions: **(1) Design inputs replaced** — the old mockups (`docs/page-designs/`), the bound design system v2, and the Claude Design V2 project are **retired as design inputs for the public shell** (they remain the workspace reference, DESIGN §14); the v3 canonical source is the shipped code (`globals.css` `:root` v3 tokens + the `v3.css` scoped remap) + the owner's reference images. This supersedes the S1 "art on white / dark ink sections" scheme **for the public shell**. **(2) Two-tier system** — v3 applies only inside `.ph-page` (+ `body:has(.ph-page)` for portals); the gated workspace deliberately keeps the green/paper system until its own sprint; never mutate a legacy token value for a v3 need. **(3) New brand mark** — the copper-arch **"Our Culture Embassy"** logo replaces the green arch in the **public chrome** (`BrandLogo` = mark PNG + real-text wordmark) and on the app-wide brand surfaces (favicon/apple-icon/OG/manifest; workspace tabs show it too — acknowledged); the workspace/admin **in-page** shells keep the green `Logo` for now (2-line follow-up when their refresh comes). Favicon is a simplified hand vector; apple-icon/OG composite the real mark PNG; theme colors = charcoal `#2A241E` / cream `#FAF6EE`. **(4) Chrome restyle sanctioned** — warm cream-glass header + charcoal footer with copper column titles, one system site-wide, plus a **transparent overlay header state on photo-hero routes** (`OVERLAY_ROUTES`, currently `/`; solidifies on scroll/menu-open) — a sanctioned two-state system, not a per-page redesign. **(5) Home copy changes (owner-approved at the DR1-4/DR1-5 gates):** hero H1 "A home for Palestinian culture, in every city." + new subline + the Café by day / Stage by night / Community every day mini-features + "Explore the model →" quiet link + the culture-split lead ("We are building a global network…") + the six "Inside a Palestine House" card labels; **removed:** the three feeling-card lines, the "how that room gets built" close, the PH-HIW stage illustrations (copper numerals instead), Home PageDividers. **`SITE_TAGLINE` + metadata/OG/footer line stay "A fixed address…" unchanged.** **(6) Photography rules** — owner's own photos only, straight-edged `.v3-photo` treatment via `photo.tsx` (no radial dissolve), optimized outputs in `public/assets/photos/` via `scripts/optimize-photos.ts` (sharp = devDependency only), masters + example mockups gitignored in `docs/source-assets/design-refs/v3/` (never committed — repo is public). **(8) DR1-9 page-heroes + mobile overrides (owner decisions, 2026-07-03):** the five key public pages (`/model`, `/experience`, `/bring-ph`, `/our-support`, `/apply`) get **v3 photo heroes now** (pulled forward from DR2 — owner-supplied masters processed into `ph-photo-{model,experience,bring-house,our-support,apply}.jpg`): a shared `PageHero` component (Home's photo/scrim/stagger grammar in a shorter band, page copy verbatim, per-photo `object-position` keeps faces in frame), those five routes added to `OVERLAY_ROUTES` (transparent header), replacing their `art-hero` + PH-* illustration heroes. **Known composition note:** the Bring-a-House master is framed chest-down (no head exists in the source) — the crop deliberately centers the coffee pour; owner may supply an alternate master later (one-line swap). **Home refinements:** hero copy nudged left into real outer whitespace only; the mini-features pinned lower (copy centers, features toward the bottom band); marquee sped to 35s + `will-change`. **Mobile (owner-directed):** the hero shows only headline → subline → CTAs (mini-features hidden ≤720px — their territory is the strip below); "Inside a Palestine House" **stacks as a plain vertical list on mobile** (no marquee/sideways scroll; desktop keeps the auto-drift).
- **(7) DR1-8 final-polish overrides (owner decisions, 2026-07-03):** **(a) The nav mega-menus are REMOVED** — supersedes the mega-menu clause of the Public-nav decision below and the pre-S13 "thumbnails removed / columns centered" clause: no panels at all; all four labels are plain links with tooltip one-liners; Live Programming surfaces via Home, the footer Explore column, and the Experience live strip. **(b) The booklet lead magnet is REMOVED ENTIRELY** — supersedes the pre-S13 "modal-only" override and retires the S12 booklet-capture touchpoint: `LeadMagnetDialog`, `LeadForm`, and `POST /api/mailchimp/booklet-capture` are deleted; Mailchimp's sole remaining use is the apply-success `applicant` tag; Resend touchpoints are untouched; the hero's second CTA is now the outline **"Explore the model"** (→ `/model`) and the platform-card line drops "Start with a free read, or"; the two public booklet PDFs stay in Storage (still featured in the gated `/resources`). `docs/EMAIL-SETUP-CHECKLIST.md` updated. **(c) The "Inside a Palestine House" strip auto-drifts** as a slow continuous CSS marquee (~60s, pauses on hover; `prefers-reduced-motion` falls back to a manual scroller with the duplicate list hidden) — a sanctioned one-section exception to the DESIGN §8 no-auto-carousel rule. **(d) The platform split ("The work is real…") sits on a photo band** — the oud-night photo **mirrored via CSS** (`scaleX(-1)`, no new asset) so its focal side lands left of the white partner card, under a charcoal scrim with white copy. **(e) Hero rebalanced** — display sizes stepped down (h1 clamp ≤3.4rem), copy staggered via the existing motion primitives, mini-features tightened. Remaining public pages render old layouts in v3 colors **by design** until DR2+. Record: `docs/sprint-prompts/dr1-public-design-refresh-v3-home.md`.

- Public nav: logo → Home + four labels (The Model · Experience · Bring a House · Our Support) + **Sign in** + green **Apply** button; hover one-liners via the Tooltip component. **The Model and Experience additionally open mega-menu panels** (two link columns + three artwork thumbnails) — built per the approved mockup chrome; the mega-menu link strings (Who's who / How it works / The rooms / More to find columns) are approved-via-mockup (owner decision, 2026-06-12). - Single CTA: "Apply to bring a House" · *Every application is reviewed by HQ.* Questions → Contact. Never "create a free account."
- **Apply = sign-up**, approval-gated: one form → pending account + application → HQ approval (`is_approved`) → unlock. No separate signup. Apply form uses a **single "Your name" field** (per mockup; resolves copy flag 5d — derive first name server-side if needed).
- **Apply Password field (owner decision, 2026-06-16, S3 3c):** the Apply form gained a **required Password field** (min 8 chars) when Apply went live. The locked mockup/copy had no password, but `/login` is password-based — letting the applicant choose their password at apply time is the reliable, standard flow (no dependence on an email link to ever sign back in). Mechanism: `signUp` (email confirmation OFF → instant session) creates the pending account, then the just-authenticated user inserts its own `pending` `applications` row under S2's owner-scoped RLS; `is_approved` stays false. `full_name` rides in signup metadata and the `0008` trigger copies it onto `profiles`. `docs/page-copy/01-public-pages/apply-partner.md` updated to match (page-copy is gitignored — OneDrive is canon).
- **Reference, not a course:** no quizzes, **no certificate**; Academy = optional gated video library; only per-user state = `/build` checklist progress.
- Stages: Plan & Prepare · Design & Build · Operate & Program. "Managing & Operating" (sidebar) and "Operate & Program" (stage) both resolve to `/operate` — intentional, not a duplicate. **Resources sits in the workspace sidebar as a primary destination** (accepted deviation, flagged in the design handoff). **(Sidebar simplified by S10 — D-S10-b, 2026-06-26: the workspace sidebar drops its group headers and flattens to Plan · Build · Operate · Live Programming · Resources › Videos / Tools & Templates, removing the duplicate "Managing & Operating" entry and the coming-soon "House Applications". The stage *content names* are unchanged; only the sidebar labels + structure simplify.)**

The Vercel `.app` domain stays live during transition.

The separate **non-production** project is created at the start of S2 — in a separate free Supabase org so it costs nothing — before any schema work; Production/Preview/Development must never share one database once schema or users exist (WORKFLOW §14). Until then, Supabase env vars are set in Vercel **Production only**; Preview/Development get the non-prod project's values at S2.
- **Repo visibility (owner decision, 2026-06-12): the GitHub repo stays public** — owner accepts the trade-off knowingly. Consequence: the gated source content currently in the repo (`docs/page-copy/06-elements/`, `docs/source-assets/resources/`) is world-readable until removed. Mitigation: that content moves out of the repo (or the repo flips private) **before launch (the new-S7 pre-launch cleanup)** — tracked in `notes/cleanup-before-launch.md`. Upside: Free-plan branch protection stays enforceable. **(Amended 2026-06-18, decision D-S5-b: deferred from the original "no later than S5" to pre-launch — the owner keeps the source in-repo for reconciliation during the content build; the public-repo exposure stays open and tracked until then.)** **(RESOLVED — S7 Step 7, 2026-06-19: verified there is NO exposure — the gated source trees (`docs/page-copy/`, `docs/page-designs/`, `docs/source-assets/`) are gitignored **and were never committed on any branch** (0 history adds), migrations are schema-only, the ingest script reads only the gitignored sources, and `.env.local` is untracked. No removal needed; the repo is safe to stay public. See `docs/notes/cleanup-before-launch.md`.)**
- **S5 full content ingestion (owner decision D-S5-a, 2026-06-18):** the complete existing content is ingested into the database + Storage **now, in full**, by a one-time idempotent script (`scripts/ingest-content.ts`), so the owner never hand-uploads. Content stays editable later, but the content-editing UI (`/admin/content`) is **V1 / post-MVP** — at MVP, content is re-ingested or edited via the Supabase dashboard. The script is a sanctioned **admin op** (reads the Supabase secret key from `.env.local`, never app code); it defaults to TEST and refuses any other project without an explicit `--i-understand-not-test` override.
- **S5 repo gated-content removal deferred (owner decision D-S5-b, 2026-06-18):** see the amended repo-visibility decision above — removal moves from "no later than S5" to **pre-launch (the new S7)**; the exposure stays tracked in `notes/cleanup-before-launch.md`.

Non-art paper-washed sections flip to the **dark ink surface** (`--ink-900`, the footer's surface) with white text via `.ph-section-dark`. Closing CTA / lead-magnet sections keep the light wash; conversion surfaces (apply/contact heroes, auth pages) stay light. Cards unify on the premium white `.ph-card` recipe (white, hairline border, soft shadow; `--lift` hover variant). This amends the mockups' section washes — do not "fix" pages back to the mockup colors. (desktop hover one-liners unchanged); (2) Home café card line is now "A café where the recipes are here to stay." (was the grandmother's-recipe line). Behavior untouched. Driven by the first post-launch feedback (workspace functionally perfect but visually plain; a senior-design review found it a notch below the live public pages). Locked design input: `DESIGN.md` (Workspace premium layer); ready-to-run prompt: `docs/sprint-prompts/s8-workspace-visual-polish.md`.

True "live" later = a YouTube **Live** link (still just an embed). Reuses the planned YouTube-only CSP.
- **D-S10-a — Approval operations simplified (owner decision, 2026-06-23):** alongside the email integrations, S10 makes HQ approvals dead-simple — an **approval/decline email** (Resend; partners are told — deferred from S4), an **admin-management UI** (add/remove/see admins so the owner never hand-edits the `admins` table again), **clearer "under review" copy** (wait expectation + "we'll email you"), and a **declined→contact path**. The approval *gate itself* is unchanged and already simple (apply → HQ clicks Approve in `/admin/approvals` → unlocks live, no re-login). **(Remapped 2026-06-26 per D-S10-b: the email + approval/decline email + declined→contact path → S12; the clearer "under review" copy → S10; the admin-management UI (add/remove admins) → S11. The simplification of HQ approvals stands; only the sprints these pieces ship in changed.)**
- **D-S8-b — Topic/workspace CTA clarity relabels (owner decision, 2026-06-24):** during S8, owner-approved label changes in the gated workspace for clarity (idiot-proof): **"Open this topic" → "Read the full guide"** (`/build`, `/plan`), **"Download templates" → "View templates"** (element action bar), **"Blocked?" → "Add notes"** (build tracker note toggle; the form hint "Blocked? Note why and move on…" and the **Blocked** status label are unchanged), and the element Templates intro reworded to "…download any you need." (templates now download **inline** on the element page via the existing approval-gated signed-URL path). Meaning preserved, behaviour unchanged. Canonical CTA copy synced in lockstep (`00-global/shared-ctas.md`, `03-member-workspace/build.md` + `plan.md` + `elements-canonical-page.md`); the 30 `06-elements/*.md` template-intro lines are reconciled in the S8 documentation pass (the gates-removal sub-step). Supersedes the verbatim wording for these specific UI strings only — do **not** "revert" the UI to the old labels.
- **D-S8-c — Retire the milestone "gates" concept site-wide (owner decision, 2026-06-24):** the "3 gates" proof number and the Day 30/60/108 "go/no-go gates / three checkpoints" journey framing are removed from the public site + docs, to keep it simple. The public proof strip's 5th stat is now **"120-day launch"** (Home + `/focus-areas`). `/bring-ph` keeps the **Day 30/60/108 milestone timeline** (Foundation / Engine working / Ready to host) but drops the "gate"/"checkpoint"/"go-no-go" language (section reframed "The 120-day launch"); the header mega-menu sub-label, Home Design & Build line, and `/our-support` link are updated to match. The proof-number invariant becomes **10 · 30 · 200+ · 267 · 120-day launch** across the governance docs (CLAUDE/AGENTS/README/ROADMAP/WORKFLOW/DESIGN + skills) and canonical page-copy, in lockstep. **Supersedes the public half of D-S6-b** (the gated workspace already never rendered gates). ⚠️ MILESTONE gate ONLY — the **approval/access gate** (`is_approved`, gated routes) and the **sprint "exit gate"** (CI checklist) are unrelated and untouched. The `checklist_items.gate` DB column is left as harmless dead schema (optional later migration); a few internal code comments still reference the old gates (cosmetic, non-user-facing).
- **D-S10-b — Post-launch Stage-3 replan + S10 scope (owner direction, 2026-06-26):** the pending plan is replanned to **S10 Private-pages clarity & polish → S11 Admin content management → S12 Email-automation placeholders + settings → S13 Full testing + fix-all (0 bugs) → S14 Final hardening + relaunch (domain + SEO)**, superseding the old pending S10 (Email & approval ops) / S11 (Journey QA) / S12 (Fix-all + Hardening). **Old→new remap:** the email integrations + the **approval/decline email** + the **declined→contact** path → **S12**; the **clearer "under review" copy** → **S10** (its private-page copy rewrite); the **admin-management UI** (add/remove admins) → **S11** (same admin panel); the journey QA + fix-all → **S13**; the hardening + final launch → **S14**. **S10 is UI / copy / curation only** and is **authorized to revise previously-locked gated-workspace decisions:** the **sidebar menu** (simplified — see the Stages decision in §4), the **stage content-curation** (a careful operate-vs-program re-grouping, **proposal-first**, all 30 topics intact), and the **per-page intro copy** (rewritten clearer per brand-voice, owner-approved each, synced to the `03-member-workspace/*` canon). **No DB / auth / RLS / approval-gate change; no public-page change.** The **approval/access gate** and the **sprint "exit gate"** are untouched. Brief: `docs/sprint-prompts/s10-private-pages-clarity.md`.

- **D-S11-a…i — Admin content management (S11) scope decisions (owner-approved at the planning gate, 2026-06-26):** the `/admin/content` CRUD panel is built behind the existing `is_admin()` gate, with these resolved decisions — **(a)** the topic/academy markdown bodies (`overview_md` / `simple_guide_md` / `watch_out_for_md` / academy `body_md`) are edited as **plain raw-markdown textareas** (text edits need no re-ingest); **(b)** markdown UX = plain textareas, **no live preview** for MVP; **(c)** **no file/video upload in the UI** — template/booklet files change via the existing `scripts/ingest-content.ts` re-ingest path; **(d)** academy scope = `youtube_url` + `body_md` only (no cover image / file upload), the URL validated with the existing `parseYouTubeId` (`src/lib/live/youtube.ts`); **(e)** `admin_remove_admin` **refuses removing yourself or the last admin** (no lockout); **(f)** the hub uses a **four-way split — Elements · Academy · Resources · Admins** (clearer for CRUD than the canon's bundled "Topics & Academy videos" / "Resources & templates"), and the canon's **"Live Programming sessions" section is dropped from `/admin/content`** because programming is already self-managed in the gated `/programming` tool (S9) — `docs/page-copy/04-admin/admin-content.md` updated to record this; **(g)** resource editing **overwrites the row's metadata in place** (no new row, no id churn); **(h)** `admin_list_admins()` exposes **email only** (joined from `auth.users`), no other PII; **(i)** upserts are **idempotent on natural keys** (`elements.slug`, `academy_modules.element_id`, `resources` by id) and a delete of a missing row is a **neutral no-op**. **UI CRUD depth (owner-approved, Q1/Q2 delegated "safest"):** Elements / Academy / Resources are **edit-only in the UI** (no Add/Delete buttons — the 30 topics + 269 files are locked content; an element delete would FK-cascade away its 1:1 academy module); **Admins** gets **Add + Remove**. The full-CRUD write RPCs (0023/0024) still ship at the DB layer for completeness + re-ingest parity. **No change to the auth/approval gates; no new env vars; no rate-limit/Turnstile (S14); the content tables + `admins` stay RLS default-deny with no client policy.**

## 5. Open decisions (resolve here, then propagate)

| # | Decision | Needed by | Options / lean | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **D-PP-k** | **The real content replaces the seeded IA.** The owner's delivered content (4 sections → 22 focus areas → 88 templates) supersedes the live 4 → 10 groups → 33 → 297. Do the 22 replace the 33, or sit alongside them — and if they replace, is the old content archived or deleted? | PP6a (scope), PP6c (execution) | Verified read-only against PROD 2026-08-14 + adversarially re-checked (5 refutation attempts, all failed): **0 of 22 focus-area names in common · 2 of 88 template names, and both are different files (19,809 B live vs 186,726 B new) · file-size ranges disjoint (live 10.5–57.5 KB, new 183–188 KB) · guide lengths disjoint (live 10,609–34,653 chars, new 1,960–4,019) · zero repo files mapping 22→33 · zero prior decisions anticipating it.** Same address space (`1.1`…`4.5`), different occupants. Lean was *keep hidden, decide at PP7*. | **RESOLVED 2026-08-14 (owner)** — **replace, and DELETE in the rollout.** PP6c's `0030` drops the old 33 `platform_topics`, the 10 superseded `platform_groups`, the 33 `elements` guide bodies and the 297 `resources` rows + Storage objects. ⚠️ **Claude's condition, accepted into scope without re-asking: a `.down.sql` restores rows but NEVER Storage objects, so PP6c must first export all 297 objects to `docs/source-assets/_archive-297-templates/` (gitignored, ~5 MB) and carry the 33 guide/overview/watch-out bodies as literal inserts in the down-migration. Backup verified — count + bytes recorded — BEFORE any DROP runs.** Owner quote: *"this is the final version (the final focus areas and content that we have)."* |
| **D-PP-l** | How are the 110 content files served — uploaded into our own private Storage, or hosted on Google Drive and linked? And `.docx` or PDF? | PP6a | Drive cannot see our approval gate: partners authenticate with Supabase, not Google, so the only workable Drive setting is **"anyone with the link"** — a permanent, public, un-revocable address for the entire partner library, reachable from page HTML. That breaks `SECURITY-CHECKLIST.md:142` (private bucket + server-issued signed URLs only) and `:138` (the search index carries no file locations), both red/blocking. `resources` also has no URL column — it stores bucket + path, both mandatory (`0014:31-32`). Size is a non-issue: 23.3 MB total, largest file 188 KB. | **RESOLVED 2026-08-14 (owner)** — **direct upload to the private `resources` bucket, signed URLs, never Drive.** Templates stay **`.docx`**: 66 of the 88 carry real tick-boxes (☐) and fill-in grids — a PDF of an empty budget template is useless. Guides + Overviews are **not files**: they become text in the DB (searchable, phone-readable, owner-editable). But `0030` deletes production's old content, so something must *create* the new content there, and the ROADMAP's phrase was only *"a reviewable SQL file plus an out-of-band Storage upload"*. | PP6c 6c-a (design) · 6c-g (build) · owner runs it | Three options were weighed. **(a) Pure SQL seed + manual upload** — `0030` carries the rows, the owner uploads 110 files through the CMS by hand. Rejected: 110 uploads through a browser form is hours of work with no verification, and it puts the riskiest step on the least-instrumented path. **(b) A separate production script** — rejected as a second implementation of a pipeline that took two blocking review rounds to get right; the bugs would be in the copy, not the original. **(c) One pipeline, two targets.** | **RESOLVED 2026-08-16 at the 6c-a kickoff — (c), with the guard rails stated up front.** `load-content.ts` gains an explicit `--target prod`, and everything about it is designed to make an accidental production write impossible rather than merely unlikely. It **requires the `PROD_*` variables by name** and **refuses to fall back** to the default client if they are absent — no silent substitution, which is precisely how the `.env.local` incident of the same morning would have become a production write under a laxer design. It **prints the full plan and demands a typed confirmation** before the first mutation, in the same preflight that already carries the `--allow-live` refusal and the code-shift detector, so a refusal still happens before anything is written. It uses the **admin's own session**, never a service key — the PP6b design point: an upload that succeeds through RLS proves the `0029` storage policies admit it, whereas one that succeeds through a service key proves nothing. And **the owner runs it**; this engine writes to production through no channel, which is unchanged. ⚠️ Deliberately **not built at 6c-a** — it is built at 6c-g, after the same pipeline has been proven end to end on TEST, so the production path is a flag on proven code rather than new code. |

Reading `src/lib/support/actions.ts` at build time disproved it: `submitSupportRequestAction` has been live since **S6**, is zod-validated, writes through the approved-only `submit_support_request` RPC (**0019**) **and emails HQ through Resend** (**S12 12-6**, `RESEND_TO_EMAIL`, reply-to = the submitter's account address). Only the **field shape** differed, and it maps with **zero backend change**: the mockup's Focus Area select → `subject`, its question textarea → `message`; name and work email are prefilled **read-only** from the session, because the account is already who HQ replies to and the action ignores anything else. **So PP3 ships the mockup's Ask HQ panel wired to the existing action, replaces the legacy form outright, and loses no channel at any point.** The mockup's two prototype lines (*"This prototype form does not send data…"*, *"In the live platform, this will go to the right HQ team."*) are dropped rather than rewritten — with a working send they describe nothing. **Lesson for the rest of Stage 4: check what is already wired before scoping it as new work.** |

The `storage_bucket` half is enforced by **PP6's 0029 RPC guard**, which already scopes exactly that check. Justification recorded so PP6 cannot forget it: **no shipped admin RPC can set `storage_bucket`** (`admin_update_resource` cannot edit `code`/`doc_key`/`storage_path` — 0023/0026), so no wrong-bucket row can be created until PP6 ships the upload path, and PROD is verified at **297 private / 0 public-in-grid / 0 wrong-bucket / 2 booklets / 0 stray-public**. **The risk window opens with PP6, and PP6's 0029 must close it.** |
| D-PP-j | **What PP4's global search actually matches.** The ROADMAP's PP4 row required this be settled at kickoff: the only guide-body read that exists is single-slug `get_element()`, so searching inside the 33 guides would mean either 33 RPC calls per query or a new search RPC — and a new migration, which renumbers PP6/PP7 and adds a production apply gate to a code-only sprint. The mockup's own `renderSearch()` matches `title + path + kind` and nothing else. | PP4 (2026-08-13) | Mockup-exact (title + path + kind) / titles + summaries / a search RPC over guide bodies | **RESOLVED 2026-08-13 (owner): titles + summaries.** A query is lowercased and split on whitespace, and **every** word must appear in the item's haystack: its **title**, its **breadcrumb path** (section label › group name, or section label › focus-area title), its **kind**, and — for a focus area only — its own **`description` + `intro`**. ① **No guide-body search**, therefore **no search RPC and no migration**: PP4 stays code-only and **PP6 keeps 0029, PP7 keeps 0030**. ② The index is the same approval-gated reads PP3 already uses (`get_platform_topics()` + `get_resources()` under the D-PP-i predicate) — 33 focus areas + 33 Simple guides + 297 templates — so a pending or anonymous caller gets an **empty index**, and it carries **no resource ids, no storage paths and no bucket names**. ③ Honest consequence, recorded rather than hidden: a hit found **only** in a summary will not highlight, because the mockup's result row shows title + path. ④ The mockup's per-hit preview modal is **not** ported (PP3 dropped it): a **Guide** hit opens the PP4 reader, a **Template** hit deep-links to `/{section}#topic-<slug>`. |

## 7. Known issues / bugs

_Log every known bug here with severity; the launch sprint (new S7) cannot pass while this section has unresolved bugs — except deferrals the owner has explicitly accepted as post-launch (e.g. #1 → the backlog hardening pass, ex-S14 — `ROADMAP.md` §A, per §5 D-S6-a)._

| # | Severity | Where | Issue | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Medium | Public writes (`/apply`, contact, `/support`) | Apply went live in S3c **without rate-limiting (Upstash) or Turnstile**; S6 adds the `/support` write under the same posture — all sequenced to the post-launch **S14 hardening** per `ROADMAP.md`. **The roadmap launched MVP-first (S7), so the site launched with these unthrottled** (bot-signup / spam-application / spam-support abuse vector). They still zod-validate and fail closed; the **HQ approval gate (`is_approved=false`) blocks any abuse from reaching gated content**, Supabase enforces email uniqueness, and the `/support` write is itself `is_approved()`-gated (approved users only). **Resolved by the backlog hardening pass** (ex-S14, retired un-started 2026-07-02 — `ROADMAP.md` §A, **required before scale**; Upstash + Turnstile + fail-closed verification); SECURITY-CHECKLIST §15 public-writes invariant is fully satisfied only then. Owner accepted this launch window (§5 D-S6-a). | Open — fix in the backlog hardening pass (ex-S14) |
| 2 | Low | `public.rls_auto_enable()` is executable by **anon** as a SECURITY DEFINER function | Surfaced by the Supabase security advisor during PP6a step 6a-c, on TEST **and** present on PROD. It is the only anon-executable SECURITY DEFINER function in the schema — every other one is revoked from `anon` (re-verified this sprint: 45 admin/member RPCs, all `anon EXECUTE = false`). It predates Stage 4 and is **not** introduced by 0029; it is a schema helper, not a data reader, so there is no known read path through it. Flagged rather than fixed because PP6a's scope is the IA write layer and changing an unrelated function's grants mid-sprint is exactly the kind of drive-by this project avoids. | ✅ **RESOLVED AND APPLIED TO PRODUCTION (2026-08-20) — migration `0034`.** The owner ran `supabase/sql/bundles/PP8_apply_prod.sql`; re-verified independently through the read-only MCP with hardened checks: production's ACL is now exactly `postgres=X/postgres | service_role=X/postgres`, **0 anon-executable `SECURITY DEFINER` functions remain**, and `ensure_rls` is still armed (`ddl_command_end`, `enabled=O`, tags `CREATE TABLE+CREATE TABLE AS+SELECT INTO`) with every table — including partitioned — RLS-enabled. ⚠️ **The obvious fix does not work and this was proven before shipping:** `revoke execute … from anon` left the privilege intact, because the ACL carried an explicit `anon` grant **and** a `PUBLIC` grant (`=X/postgres`) that `anon` inherits — it would have applied cleanly, reported success and closed nothing. `0034` revokes `from public, anon, authenticated`. The real risk was never the grant: `rls_auto_enable()` returns `event_trigger` and backs the **`ensure_rls`** trigger that auto-enables RLS on every new `public` table, so breaking it would have been far worse than the finding. Proven safe on TEST — after the revoke, `anon` is denied (`42501`), **0** anon-executable `SECURITY DEFINER` functions remain, and a freshly created table still came out `relrowsecurity = true`. The down-migration was executed too and restores all three ACL entries. |
| 3 | Low | `/admin/content` puts its own four card labels + link paths into the RSC payload of an ANONYMOUS request | Found by probing the live site after the PP6a merge, 2026-08-15. The gate itself is correct and works: `src/app/admin/layout.tsx` redirects an anonymous caller to `/login`, and the browser lands there. But Next.js renders the page segment in PARALLEL with the layout, so the hub's output is already in the streamed flight data when the layout's `redirect()` resolves — the response is a 200 carrying both. **What is exposed is only structure, never data:** the four hardcoded labels (Pages · Focus areas · Files · Admins) and their `/admin/content/*` paths. Verified clean on every data-bearing route — `/admin/content/{focus-areas,files,pages}` and `/admin/approvals` return **no** topic titles, ids, storage paths or emails to an anonymous caller, and `/setup` and `/dashboard` return no focus-area or partner data at all, because the RPCs behind them fail closed. Pre-dates PP6a architecturally (the hub is from S11); PP6a only renamed the labels, which is why it is newly visible. | ✅ **RESOLVED in PP8 (2026-08-20).** Reproduced on **live production** first — an anonymous `GET /admin/content` returned 200 carrying all four labels and paths in the HTML *and* the RSC payload. `ContentAdminPage` is now `async` and calls `isAdmin()` itself, which both adds the second gate CLAUDE.md requires of every route and makes the render depend on a server round-trip — the part that actually stops the segment streaming ahead of the layout's `redirect()`. Verified anonymous: **0 leaked strings in HTML and 0 in RSC**, with every other admin and gated route swept clean alongside; and verified from a real **admin** session that the hub still renders with all four cards. ⚠️ **The first fix was right and its RECORDED REASON was wrong, which cost five more routes — caught by the 8-k independent review.** The note claimed awaiting `isAdmin()` worked because it "makes the render depend on a server round-trip". It does not: `/admin/content/files` awaits `searchParams` **and two gated RPCs** and still flushed its own heading to an anonymous caller, as did `/admin/approvals`, `/pages`, `/admins` and `/focus-areas`. **What closes it is `notFound()` throwing before the JSX is constructed.** The original source sweep was scoped by that false rule and under-counted ("one other" — it was five). **All six admin pages now gate themselves before producing JSX; re-verified 0 own-string hits in HTML and RSC anonymously, and all six still render for a real admin.** Data was never exposed at any point — the gated RPCs fail closed, so the payloads carried headings and intros but no topic title, storage path or applicant email. |

| 4 | Low | `/admin/content/files` — the owner's own upload | **A `.docx` upload was refused with "That file type isn't supported"** while the owner was working the CMS at the PP6b pilot, 2026-08-15. **Not reproduced.** Both checks are pure extension tests (`files-admin.tsx`, `file-actions.ts` — client and server say the same thing), a real delivered `.docx` passes the client check when driven in a browser, and the loader uploads the same bytes through the same RPC without complaint. The two live hypotheses are a **`.doc`** (indistinguishable from `.docx` in a picker with extensions hidden) and a **trailing space in the filename** (allowed by OneDrive and Windows); the second is now absorbed by a `.trim()`, and the message names the extension it actually received, so the next occurrence diagnoses itself in one attempt. **Not blocking PP6c:** the owner's decision at D-PP-q ③ is that the 110 files are uploaded by the ingest pipeline, not by hand. | Open — self-diagnosing; close it when the owner next uploads by hand and reports what the message says |

Two more FK facts shaped the deletion order, both checked before writing a line: `resources.element_id` is **ON DELETE SET NULL**, so deleting elements first would have *orphaned* the 297 rows rather than removed them, and `platform_topics.group_id` is **ON DELETE RESTRICT**, so groups cannot precede their topics. Committing it would publish the entire private corpus in one commit and break the S7 Step 7 invariant that no gated content has ever entered this repo. So `docs/archive-297-manifest.json` is **committed and carries no object paths** (count, bytes, fingerprint, provenance, and how to re-check), while the full 297 rows sit beside the bytes in the gitignored archive and can be regenerated by re-running the script. |

| 2026-06-27 | **S11 independent (Codex) review + fix.** One **blocking** finding: `admin_remove_admin` checked `count(*) <= 1` without locking `public.admins`, so two admins removing **each other** concurrently could both pass the last-admin guard and both delete → an empty admins table (lockout, a TOCTOU race — my serial-only reasoning had missed the concurrent case). **Fixed** by serializing removals: `lock table public.admins in share row exclusive mode` before the count read (the lock conflicts with itself, so a second concurrent removal waits then re-reads an accurate count; plain `is_admin`/`is_approved` reads take ACCESS SHARE and are not blocked). 0024 was **TEST-only** (not yet on PROD), so the fix is a `create or replace` in the same `0024_admin_management_rpcs.up.sql` — **re-applied + re-verified on TEST** (lock present; self-removal still blocked; positive add+remove still works). Verify scripts gained a structural lock assertion (TEST §12b + PROD-safe §7). Reviewer otherwise: admin/RPC gating clean, no secret/RLS/boundary issues; typecheck/lint/build pass. ⚠️ **If 0024 was already applied to PROD, this needs a new 0025 instead — confirm before PROD apply.** |
